     9→import { syncOwnedCharacters } from '../utils/gacha';
    10→
    11→// 그리드 설정
    12→const COLS = 3;
    13→const CARD_W = 100;
    14→const CARD_H = 120;
    15→const GAP_X = 15;
    16→const GAP_Y = 10;
    17→const GRID_LEFT = (400 - (COLS * CARD_W + (COLS - 1) * GAP_X)) / 2; // 35px
    18→const GRID_TOP = 145;
    19→
    20→// 스크롤 영역 (헤더 아래 ~ 하단 버튼 위)
    21→const SCROLL_TOP = 128;
    22→const SCROLL_BOTTOM = 548;
    23→
    24→export default class CharacterSelectScene extends Phaser.Scene {
    25→  private selectedId: string = 'chibi';
    26→  private ownedIds: string[] = [];
    27→  private cardHighlights: Map<string, Phaser.GameObjects.Rectangle> = new Map();
    28→
    29→  // 스크롤
    30→  private cardsContainer!: Phaser.GameObjects.Container;
    31→  private scrollOffset = 0;
    32→  private maxScrollOffset = 0;
    33→  private pointerDownY = 0;
    34→  private pointerDownScrollY = 0;
    35→  private hasDragged = false;
    36→
    37→  // 동적 갱신용 ref
    38→  private headerNameText!: Phaser.GameObjects.Text;
    39→  private bgImage!: Phaser.GameObjects.Image;
    40→
    41→  // 상세 정보 패널
    42→  private detailPanel: Phaser.GameObjects.Container | null = null;
    43→  private infoPanel: Phaser.GameObjects.Container | null = null;
    44→
    45→  constructor() {
    46→    super('CharacterSelectScene');
    47→  }
    48→
    49→  preload() {
    50→    for (const char of CHARACTERS) {
    51→      if (!this.textures.exists(char.imageKey)) {
    52→        this.load.image(char.imageKey, char.imagePath);
    53→      }
    54→      if (!this.textures.exists(char.illustKey)) {
    55→        this.load.image(char.illustKey, char.illustPath);
    56→      }
    57→      if (char.videoKey && char.videoPath && !this.cache.video.exists(char.videoKey)) {
    58→        this.load.video(char.videoKey, char.videoPath);
    59→      }
    60→    }
    61→  }
    62→
    63→  create() {
    64→    this.selectedId = getSelectedCharacter();
    65→    this.ownedIds = getOwnedCharacters();
    66→    this.scrollOffset = 0;
    67→    this.hasDragged = false;
    68→    this.cardHighlights.clear();
    69→
    70→    // 서버 DB와 동기화 — 소유 목록이 바뀐 경우에만 씬 재시작해서 카드 갱신
    71→    syncOwnedCharacters().then(synced => {
    72→      if (!this.scene.isActive()) return;
    73→      const changed = synced.length !== this.ownedIds.length ||
    74→        synced.some(id => !this.ownedIds.includes(id));
    75→      if (changed) this.scene.restart();
    76→    }).catch(() => { /* 네트워크 오류 시 로컬 상태 유지 */ });
    77→
    78→    const selectedDef = CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === this.selectedId) ?? CHARACTERS[0];
    79→
    80→    // ── 배경: 선택된 캐릭터 일러스트 ───────────────────────────────────
    81→    this.bgImage = this.add.image(200, 300, selectedDef.illustKey);
    82→    this.bgImage.setDisplaySize(400, 600);
    83→
    84→    // ── 헤더 (고정) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    85→    this.add.text(200, 40, '캐릭터 선택', {
    86→      fontSize: '26px',
    87→      color: '#ffffff',
    88→      fontStyle: 'bold',
    89→      stroke: '#000',
    90→      strokeThickness: 4,
    91→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
    92→
    93→    this.headerNameText = this.add.text(200, 80, `현재: ${selectedDef.name}`, {
    94→      fontSize: '16px',
    95→      color: selectedDef.gradeColor,
    96→      stroke: '#000',
    97→      strokeThickness: 3,
    98→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
    99→
   100→    this.add.text(200, 108, '수집한 캐릭터를 선택하세요', {
   101→      fontSize: '13px',
   102→      color: '#888888',
   103→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   104→
   105→    // ── 스크롤 가능한 카드 컨테이너 ─────────────────────────────────────
   106→    this.cardsContainer = this.add.container(0, 0);
   107→
   108→    CHARACTERS.forEach((char, index) => {
   109→      const col = index % COLS;
   110→      const row = Math.floor(index / COLS);
   111→      const x = GRID_LEFT + col * (CARD_W + GAP_X) + CARD_W / 2;
   112→      const y = GRID_TOP + row * (CARD_H + GAP_Y) + CARD_H / 2;
   113→      this.createCharacterCard(char, x, y);
   114→    });
   115→
   116→    // 스크롤 최대 범위 계산
   117→    const totalRows = Math.ceil(CHARACTERS.length / COLS);
   118→    const contentBottom = GRID_TOP + (totalRows - 1) * (CARD_H + GAP_Y) + CARD_H + 10;
   119→    this.maxScrollOffset = Math.max(0, contentBottom - SCROLL_BOTTOM);
   120→
   121→    // 카드 영역 마스크 (스크롤 영역 밖 숨김)
   122→    const maskGfx = this.make.graphics({ x: 0, y: 0 });
   123→    maskGfx.fillStyle(0xffffff);
   124→    maskGfx.fillRect(0, SCROLL_TOP, 400, SCROLL_BOTTOM - SCROLL_TOP);
   125→    this.cardsContainer.setMask(maskGfx.createGeometryMask());
   126→
   127→    // ── 드래그 스크롤 입력 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   128→    this.input.on('pointerdown', (p: Phaser.Input.Pointer) => {
   129→      if (this.detailPanel) return; // 상세 패널 열려있으면 스크롤 무시
   130→      if (p.y < SCROLL_TOP || p.y > SCROLL_BOTTOM) return;
   131→      this.pointerDownY = p.y;
   132→      this.pointerDownScrollY = this.scrollOffset;
   133→      this.hasDragged = false;
   134→    });
   135→
   136→    this.input.on('pointermove', (p: Phaser.Input.Pointer) => {
   137→      if (this.detailPanel) return; // 상세 패널 열려있으면 스크롤 무시
   138→      if (!p.isDown) return;
   139→      const dy = this.pointerDownY - p.y;
   140→      if (Math.abs(dy) > 5) {
   141→        this.hasDragged = true;
   142→        this.scrollOffset = Phaser.Math.Clamp(
   143→          this.pointerDownScrollY + dy,
   144→          0,
   145→          this.maxScrollOffset,
   146→        );
   147→        this.cardsContainer.setY(-this.scrollOffset);
   148→      }
   149→    });
   150→
   151→    this.input.on('pointerup', () => {
   152→      if (this.detailPanel) return; // 상세 패널 열려있으면 hasDragged 초기화 무시
   153→      // card pointerup 이벤트가 먼저 발생하므로 다음 프레임에 초기화
   154→      this.time.delayedCall(0, () => { this.hasDragged = false; });
   155→    });
   156→
   157→    // 마우스 휠
   158→    this.input.on(
   159→      'wheel',
   160→      (_p: Phaser.Input.Pointer, _go: unknown[], _dx: number, deltaY: number) => {
   161→        if (this.detailPanel) return; // 상세 패널 열려있으면 휠 스크롤 무시
   162→        this.scrollOffset = Phaser.Math.Clamp(
   163→          this.scrollOffset + deltaY * 0.5,
   164→          0,
   165→          this.maxScrollOffset,
   166→        );
   167→        this.cardsContainer.setY(-this.scrollOffset);
   168→      },
   169→    );
   170→
   171→    // ── 고정 UI ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   172→    this.createBackButton();
   173→  }
   174→
   175→  private createCharacterCard(char: CharacterDef, x: number, y: number) {
   176→    const isOwned = this.ownedIds.includes(char.id);
   177→    const isSelected = this.selectedId === char.id;
   178→    const gradeColorInt = parseInt(char.gradeColor.replace('#', ''), 16);
   179→
   180→    // 카드 배경
   181→    const cardBg = this.add.rectangle(x, y, CARD_W, CARD_H, 0x222222);
   182→    cardBg.setStrokeStyle(2, isOwned ? gradeColorInt : 0x444444);
   183→    this.cardsContainer.add(cardBg);
   184→
   185→    // 선택 하이라이트
   186→    const highlight = this.add.rectangle(x, y, CARD_W, CARD_H, 0, 0);
   187→    highlight.setStrokeStyle(3, 0xffffff);
   188→    highlight.setVisible(isSelected);
   189→    this.cardHighlights.set(char.id, highlight);
   190→    this.cardsContainer.add(highlight);
   191→
   192→    // 캐릭터 이미지
   193→    const img = this.add.image(x, y - 12, char.imageKey);
   194→    img.setDisplaySize(58, 85);
   195→    this.cardsContainer.add(img);
   196→
   197→    if (!isOwned) {
   198→      img.setTint(0x000000);
   199→      img.setAlpha(0.6);
   200→      const lock = this.add.text(x, y - 12, '🔒', { fontSize: '22px' }).setOrigin(0.5);
   201→      this.cardsContainer.add(lock);
   202→    }
   203→
   204→    // 등급 배지
   205→    const badge = this.add.text(x + CARD_W / 2 - 2, y - CARD_H / 2 + 2, char.grade, {
   206→      fontSize: '9px',
   207→      color: char.gradeColor,
   208→      fontStyle: 'bold',
   209→      backgroundColor: '#000000cc',
   210→      padding: { x: 3, y: 1 },
   211→    }).setOrigin(1, 0);
   212→    this.cardsContainer.add(badge);
   213→
   214→    // 캐릭터 이름
   215→    const nameText = this.add.text(x, y + CARD_H / 2 - 16, char.name, {
   216→      fontSize: '12px',
   217→      color: isOwned ? '#ffffff' : '#555555',
   218→      fontStyle: 'bold',
   219→    }).setOrigin(0.5);
   220→    this.cardsContainer.add(nameText);
   221→
   222→    // 모든 카드 클릭 가능 (탭 → 상세 패널 열기)
   223→    cardBg.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: isOwned });
   224→
   225→    if (isOwned) {
   226→      cardBg.on('pointerover', () => {
   227→        if (this.selectedId !== char.id) cardBg.setFillStyle(0x333333);
   228→      });
   229→      cardBg.on('pointerout', () => {
   230→        if (this.selectedId !== char.id) cardBg.setFillStyle(0x222222);
   231→      });
   232→    }
   233→    // pointerup으로 상세 패널 열기 (드래그 스크롤과 구분)
   234→    cardBg.on('pointerup', () => {
   235→      if (!this.hasDragged) this.showCharacterDetail(char.id);
   236→    });
   237→  }
   238→
   239→  private selectCharacter(id: string) {
   240→    // 이전 선택 하이라이트 제거
   241→    const prev = this.cardHighlights.get(this.selectedId);
   242→    if (prev) prev.setVisible(false);
   243→
   244→    this.selectedId = id;
   245→    setSelectedCharacter(id);
   246→
   247→    const next = this.cardHighlights.get(id);
   248→    if (next) next.setVisible(true);
   249→
   250→    // 헤더 직접 갱신 (씬 재시작 없이)
   251→    const def = CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === id) ?? CHARACTERS[0];
   252→    this.headerNameText.setText(`현재: ${def.name}`);
   253→    this.headerNameText.setColor(def.gradeColor);
   254→    this.bgImage.setTexture(def.illustKey);
   255→  }
   256→
   257→  private showCharacterDetail(id: string): void {
   258→    this.hideCharacterDetail();
   259→
   260→    const def = CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === id) ?? CHARACTERS[0];
   261→    const isOwned = this.ownedIds.includes(id);
   262→    const gradeColorInt = parseInt(def.gradeColor.replace('#', ''), 16);
   263→
   264→    const panel = this.add.container(0, 0).setDepth(300);
   265→    this.detailPanel = panel;
   266→
   267→    // ── 일러스트 전체 화면 ───────────────────────────────────────────
   268→    const illust = this.add.image(200, 300, def.illustKey).setDisplaySize(400, 600);
   269→    panel.add(illust);
   270→
   271→    // ── 비디오 (일러스트와 같은 위치, 처음엔 숨김) ─────────────────
   272→    const hasVideo = !!(def.videoKey && this.cache.video.exists(def.videoKey));
   273→    const videoObj = hasVideo
   274→      ? this.add.video(200, 300, def.videoKey!).setDisplaySize(400, 600).setVisible(false)
   275→      : null;
   276→    if (videoObj) panel.add(videoObj);
   277→
   278→    // ── 하단 그라디언트 (위 투명 → 아래 짙은 어둠) ─────────────────
   279→    // 비디오/일러스트 위, 버튼 아래에 위치하도록 여기서 추가
   280→    const grad = this.add.graphics();
   281→    grad.fillGradientStyle(0x000000, 0x000000, 0x000000, 0x000000, 0, 0, 0.78, 0.78);
   282→    grad.fillRect(0, 380, 400, 220);
   283→    panel.add(grad);
   284→
   285→    // ── ✕ 닫기 버튼 (우상단 플로팅) ────────────────────────────────
   286→    const closeBg = this.add.circle(372, 38, 18, 0x000000, 0.55);
   287→    const closeBtn = this.add.text(372, 38, '✕', {
   288→      fontSize: '18px', color: '#cccccc',
   289→    }).setOrigin(0.5).setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   290→    closeBtn.on('pointerover', () => closeBtn.setColor('#ffffff'));
   291→    closeBtn.on('pointerout',  () => closeBtn.setColor('#cccccc'));
   292→    closeBtn.on('pointerup',   () => this.hideCharacterDetail());
   293→    panel.add(closeBg);
   294→    panel.add(closeBtn);
   295→
   296→    // ── 하단 정보 영역 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   297→    // 픽셀 스프라이트
   298→    const sprite = this.add.image(38, 515, def.imageKey).setDisplaySize(46, 64);
   299→    if (!isOwned) { sprite.setTint(0x222222).setAlpha(0.55); }
   300→    panel.add(sprite);
   301→
   302→    // 등급 배지

   307→    panel.add(gradeBadge);
   308→
   309→    // 캐릭터 이름 (크게)
   310→    const nameText = this.add.text(74, 510, def.name, {
   311→      fontSize: '26px', color: '#ffffff', fontStyle: 'bold',
   312→      stroke: '#000000', strokeThickness: 5,
   313→    });
   314→    panel.add(nameText);
   315→
   316→    // ── 하단 버튼 2개 (나란히) ──────────────────────────────────────
   317→    const BTN_Y = 568;

   320→
   321→    // 📋 정보 보기 (좌)
   322→    const infoBg = this.add.rectangle(108, BTN_Y, BTN_W, BTN_H, 0x1a1a1a)

   324→      .setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   325→    infoBg.on('pointerover', () => infoBg.setFillStyle(0x2e2e2e));

   327→    infoBg.on('pointerup',   () => this.showInfoPanel(def));
   328→    const infoLabel = this.add.text(108, BTN_Y, '📋  정보 보기', {

   331→    panel.add(infoBg);
   332→    panel.add(infoLabel);
   333→
   334→    // ✔ 선택하기 / 🔒 미보유 (우)
   335→    if (isOwned) {
   336→      const selBg = this.add.rectangle(292, BTN_Y, BTN_W, BTN_H, 0x1144bb)

   353→      }).setOrigin(0.5);
   354→      panel.add(lockBg);
   355→      panel.add(lockLabel);
   356→    }
   357→
   358→    // ── 비디오 인터랙션 (비디오가 있는 캐릭터만) ────────────────────
   359→    if (hasVideo && videoObj) {
   360→      // 일러스트 탭 → 비디오 재생 (가챠와 동일한 fitVideo 방식)
   361→      // active 체크: ✕ 탭 시 패널 파괴 직후 이 핸들러가 동시에 발화하는 경우 방지
   362→      const startVideo = () => {
   363→        if (!illust.active || !videoObj?.active) return;
   364→        illust.setVisible(false);
   365→        videoObj!.setVisible(true);
   366→        videoObj!.play(false);
   367→        this.fitVideoToPanel(videoObj!);
   368→      };
   369→      illust.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   370→      illust.on('pointerup', startVideo);
   371→
   372→      // 비디오 탭 또는 재생 완료 → 일러스트로 복귀
   373→      const stopVideo = () => {
   374→        videoObj!.stop();
   375→        videoObj!.setVisible(false);
   376→        illust.setVisible(true);
   377→      };
   378→      videoObj.setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });
   379→      videoObj.on('pointerup', stopVideo);
   380→      videoObj.on('complete', stopVideo);
   381→    }
   382→  }
   383→
   384→  /** GachaScene.fitVideoToCanvas와 동일 — aspect ratio 유지하며 400×600 캔버스에 cover */
   385→  private fitVideoToPanel(vid: Phaser.GameObjects.Video): void {
   386→    const W = 400, H = 600;
   387→    vid.setDisplaySize(W, H);
   388→
   389→    const applyContain = () => {
   390→      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
   391→      const el: HTMLVideoElement | null = (vid as any).video ?? null;
   392→      const nw = el?.videoWidth  || 0;
   393→      const nh = el?.videoHeight || 0;
   394→      if (nw > 0 && nh > 0) {
   395→        const scale = Math.max(W / nw, H / nh);
   396→        vid.setDisplaySize(Math.round(nw * scale), Math.round(nh * scale));
   397→      }
   398→    };
   399→
   400→    vid.once('play', applyContain);
   401→    this.time.delayedCall(100, applyContain);
   402→    this.time.delayedCall(500, applyContain);
   403→  }
   404→

   406→    this.hideInfoPanel();
   407→    this.detailPanel?.destroy();
   408→    this.detailPanel = null;

   467→    }).setOrigin(0.5).setInteractive({ useHandCursor: true });

> AGENT

두 가지 문제가 명확합니다.

1. **클릭 투과**: `detailPanel`에 전체화면 차단 레이어가 없어서 아래 카드까지 클릭이 전달됨
2. **X 버튼 영역**: `closeBg`(circle)는 인터랙티브 없고, `closeBtn`(텍스트)만 인터랙티브라 텍스트 범위만 클릭 가능

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- Phaser 텍스트의 히트 영역은 렌더된 글자 크기만큼이다. `✕` 한 글자는 약 14×14px 정도라 버튼처럼 느껴지지 않는다. circle 자체를 인터랙티브로 만들어야 한다.
- Container는 입력 이벤트를 막지 않는다. 전체 화면을 덮는 투명 Rectangle을 패널 맨 아래에 깔아야 아래 오브젝트로 이벤트가 전달되지 않는다.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

현재 전체 사용자에게 스코르 2만개씩 넣어주려고 하는데 쿼리를 알려줘. user_skor 테이블에 넣으면 될 듯 한데

> AGENT

`user_skor` 행이 없는 신규 유저까지 한 번에 처리하는 쿼리입니다.

```sql
-- user_skor 행이 있으면 +20000, 없으면 20000으로 신규 생성
INSERT INTO user_skor (user_id, balance)
SELECT id, 20000
FROM auth.users
ON CONFLICT (user_id)
DO UPDATE SET balance = user_skor.balance + 20000;
```

단순 UPDATE만 쓰면 `user_skor` 테이블에 행이 없는 유저(가입 후 SKOR를 한 번도 받지 않은 경우)는 누락됩니다. 위 쿼리는 `auth.users` 전체를 기준으로 처리해서 빠짐없이 적용됩니다.

Supabase 대시보드 → **SQL Editor**에서 실행하면 됩니다.

> DEVELOPER

     1→import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
     2→
     3→const corsHeaders = {
     4→  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
     5→  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
     6→};
     7→
     8→// SKOR 가중치
     9→const WEIGHTS = {
    10→  gold: 0.1,
    11→  diamond: 0.3,
    12→  topaz: 0.7,
    13→  rainbow: 2.0,
    14→};
    15→
    16→// 점수 브래킷별 게임당 SKOR 상한
    17→function getBracketCap(score: number): number {
    18→  if (score < 1000) return 8;
    19→  if (score < 2000) return 14;
    20→  if (score < 3000) return 18;
    21→  return 22;
    22→}
    23→
    24→// 주간 SKOR 상한
    25→const WEEKLY_CAP = 200;
    26→
    27→// 반복 퀘스트 정의
    28→const QUESTS = [
    29→  { key: 'gold',    interval: 300, reward: 5  },
    30→  { key: 'diamond', interval: 150, reward: 8  },
    31→  { key: 'topaz',   interval: 80,  reward: 10 },
    32→  { key: 'rainbow', interval: 50,  reward: 15 },
    33→] as const;
    34→
    35→Deno.serve(async (req: Request) => {
    36→  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    37→    return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
    38→  }
    39→
    40→  try {
    41→    const {
    42→      score,
    43→      goldCollected = 0,
    44→      diamondCollected = 0,
    45→      topazCollected = 0,
    46→      rainbowCollected = 0,
    47→    } = await req.json();
    48→
    49→    if (typeof score !== 'number' || score < 0) {
    50→      return new Response(
    51→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid score' }),
    52→        { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    53→      );
    54→    }
    55→
    56→    // 인증
    57→    const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization');
    58→    if (!authHeader) {
    59→      return new Response(
    60→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing authorization header' }),
    61→        { status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    62→      );
    63→    }
    64→
    65→    const supabaseUser = createClient(
    66→      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '',
    67→      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_ANON_KEY') ?? '',
    68→      { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } }
    69→    );
    70→
    71→    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabaseUser.auth.getUser();
    72→    if (authError || !user) {
    73→      return new Response(
    74→        JSON.stringify({ error: 'Unauthorized' }),
    75→        { status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    76→      );
    77→    }
    78→
    79→    const supabaseAdmin = createClient(
    80→      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '',
    81→      Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY') ?? ''
    82→    );
    83→
    84→    // ── 1. 게임 수익 SKOR 계산 ─────────────────────────────────────
    85→    const rawSkor =
    86→      goldCollected * WEIGHTS.gold +
    87→      diamondCollected * WEIGHTS.diamond +
    88→      topazCollected * WEIGHTS.topaz +
    89→      rainbowCollected * WEIGHTS.rainbow;
    90→
    91→    const bracketCap = getBracketCap(score);
    92→    const gameSkor = Math.min(rawSkor, bracketCap);
    93→
    94→    // ── 2. 퀘스트 진행도 업데이트 및 보상 계산 ─────────────────────
    95→    const { data: existingProgress } = await supabaseAdmin
    96→      .from('quest_progress')
    97→      .select('*')
    98→      .eq('user_id', user.id)
    99→      .single();
   100→
   101→    const prevProgress = existingProgress ?? {
   102→      gold_total: 0, diamond_total: 0, topaz_total: 0, rainbow_total: 0
   103→    };
   104→
   105→    const newProgress = {
   106→      gold_total:    prevProgress.gold_total    + goldCollected,
   107→      diamond_total: prevProgress.diamond_total + diamondCollected,
   108→      topaz_total:   prevProgress.topaz_total   + topazCollected,
   109→      rainbow_total: prevProgress.rainbow_total + rainbowCollected,
   110→    };
   111→
   112→    const newQuestRewards: { quest: string; reward: number }[] = [];
   113→    let questSkor = 0;
   114→
   115→    for (const quest of QUESTS) {
   116→      const prevTotal = prevProgress[`${quest.key}_total` as keyof typeof prevProgress] as number;
   117→      const newTotal  = newProgress[`${quest.key}_total` as keyof typeof newProgress] as number;
   118→
   119→      const prevMilestone = Math.floor(prevTotal / quest.interval);
   120→      const newMilestone  = Math.floor(newTotal  / quest.interval);
   121→
   122→      const achieved = newMilestone - prevMilestone;
   123→      if (achieved > 0) {
   124→        const reward = achieved * quest.reward;
   125→        newQuestRewards.push({ quest: quest.key, reward });
   126→        questSkor += reward;
   127→      }
   128→    }
   129→
   130→    // ── 3. 주간 캡 적용 ────────────────────────────────────────────
   131→    const { data: existingSkor } = await supabaseAdmin
   132→      .from('user_skor')
   133→      .select('*')
   134→      .eq('user_id', user.id)
   135→      .single();
   136→
   137→    const now = new Date();
   138→    const currentWeekStart = getWeekStart(now);
   139→
   140→    let weeklyEarned = existingSkor?.weekly_earned ?? 0;
   141→    const storedWeekStart = existingSkor?.week_start;
   142→
   143→    // 주간 리셋 체크
   144→    if (!storedWeekStart || storedWeekStart < currentWeekStart) {
   145→      weeklyEarned = 0;
   146→    }
   147→
   148→    const weeklyRemaining = Math.max(0, WEEKLY_CAP - weeklyEarned);
   149→    const totalRaw = gameSkor + questSkor;
   150→    const totalSkorAdded = Math.min(totalRaw, weeklyRemaining);
   151→
   152→    const currentBalance = existingSkor?.balance ?? 0;
   153→    const newBalance = currentBalance + totalSkorAdded;
   154→
   155→    // ── 4. DB 업데이트 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   156→    await supabaseAdmin
   157→      .from('user_skor')
   158→      .upsert({
   159→        user_id: user.id,
   160→        balance: Math.floor(newBalance),
   161→        weekly_earned: Math.floor(weeklyEarned + totalSkorAdded),
   162→        week_start: currentWeekStart,
   163→        updated_at: now.toISOString(),
   164→      }, { onConflict: 'user_id' });
   165→
   166→    await supabaseAdmin
   167→      .from('quest_progress')
   168→      .upsert({ user_id: user.id, ...newProgress }, { onConflict: 'user_id' });
   169→
   170→    return new Response(
   171→      JSON.stringify({
   172→        success: true,
   173→        skorEarned: Math.floor(gameSkor),
   174→        bracketCap,
   175→        questRewards: newQuestRewards,
   176→        totalSkorAdded: Math.floor(totalSkorAdded),
   177→        remainingBalance: Math.floor(newBalance),
   178→        weeklyCapRemaining: Math.floor(weeklyRemaining - totalSkorAdded),
   179→      }),
   180→      { headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
   181→    );
   182→  } catch (error) {
   183→    console.error('Unexpected error:', error);
   184→    return new Response(
   185→      JSON.stringify({ error: 'Internal server error' }),
   186→      { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
   187→    );
   188→  }
   189→});
   190→
   191→// 해당 날짜가 속한 주의 월요일(YYYY-MM-DD) 반환
   192→function getWeekStart(date: Date): string {
   193→  const d = new Date(date);
   194→  const day = d.getDay(); // 0=일, 1=월 ... 6=토
   195→  const diff = (day === 0 ? -6 : 1 - day); // 월요일로 이동
   196→  d.setDate(d.getDate() + diff);
   197→  return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
   198→}
